Cavity operators\n    a = tensor(destroy(nmax), qeye(2**N))\n\n    # Spin operators (collective)\n    Jz_list = [tensor(qeye(nmax), *[sigmaz() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                     for i in range(N)]) / 2\n               for j in range(N)]\n    Jp_list = [tensor(qeye(nmax), *[sigmax() + 1j*sigmay() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                     for i in range(N)]) / 2\n               for j in range(N)]\n    Jm_list = [tensor(qeye(nmax), *[sigmax() - 1j*sigmay() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                     for i in range(N)]) / 2\n               for j in range(N)]\n\n    # Collective emission (sigma- for all spins)\n    Jm_coll = sum([tensor(qeye(nmax), *[sigmax() - 1j*sigmay() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                         for i in range(N)])/2 for j in range(N)])\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down_coll) * Jm_coll)\n\n    # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef trace_out_spins(rho_ss, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Trace out the spin degrees of freedom and extract the cavity state.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    rho_ss : Qobj\n        Steady state density matrix\n    nmax : int\n        Photon number cutoff\n    N : int\n        Number of spins\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Reduced cavity density matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Trace out spin degrees of freedom (keep only cavity)\n    rho_cavity = ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n    return rho_cavity\n\ndef calculate_wigner_grid(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Wigner function on a grid.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Cavity state (density matrix or ket)\n    xvec : array\n        x (position) grid points\n    pvec : array\n        p (momentum) grid points\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    W : 2D array\n        Wigner function values on the grid\n    \"\"\"\n    W = wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n    return W\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"\n    Save Wigner function as CSV file.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    W : 2D array\n        Wigner function values\n    xvec : array\n        x grid points\n    pvec : array\n        p grid points\n    filename : str\n        Output filename\n    \"\"\"\n    # Create DataFrame with proper indexing\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = 'p'\n    df.columns.name = 'x'\n    df.to_csv(filename)\n    print(f\"Saved {filename}\")\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Open Dicke Model - Steady State and Wigner Function Calculation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Parameters\n    N = 4\n    omega0 = 1.0\n    omegac = 1.0\n    g = 2.0 / np.sqrt(N)\n    kappa = 1.0\n    nmax = 16\n\n    # Loss parameters\n    gamma_phi = 0.01\n    gamma_up = 0.1\n    gamma_down = 0.1\n    gamma_up_coll = 0.1\n    gamma_down_coll = 0.1\n\n    # Wigner function grid\n    xvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n    pvec = np.linspace(-6, 6, 1000)\n\n    print(f\"\\nModel Parameters:\")\n    print(f\"  N (2-level systems): {N}\")\n    print(f\"  ω₀ (atomic frequency): {omega0}\")\n    print(f\"  ωc (cavity frequency): {omegac}\")\n    print(f\"  g (coupling): {g:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"  κ (cavity loss): {kappa}\")\n    print(f\"  nmax (photon cutoff): {nmax}\")\n    print(f\"  Wigner grid: 1000 × 1000 points in [-6, 6] × [-6, 6]\")\n\n    # Set up Hamiltonian and operators\n    print(f\"\\nSetting up Dicke model...\")\n    H, a, Jz, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list = setup_dicke_model(N, omega0, omegac, g, nmax)\n\n    # Case 1: Local de-phasing & local pumping\n    print(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    print(\"Case 1: Local de-phasing & local pumping\")\n    print(f\"  γ_φ = {gamma_phi}, γ_↑ = {gamma_up}\")\n    print(f\"{'='*70}\")\n    print(\"Computing steady state...\")\n    rho_ss_1 = compute_steady_state_case1(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_up, nmax, N)\n    print(\"Tracing out spins...\")\n    rho_cavity_1 = trace_out_spins(rho_ss_1, nmax, N)\n    print(\"Calculating Wigner function...\")\n    W_1 = calculate_wigner_grid(rho_cavity_1, xvec, pvec)\n    print(\"Saving results...\")\n    save_wigner_csv(W_1, xvec, pvec, \"1.csv\")\n\n    # Build list of operators for tensor product\n    # First is cavity, rest are spins\n    cavity_ops = [destroy(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity destruction operator\n    a = tensor(cavity_ops)\n\n    # Cavity identity\n    cavity_id = tensor([qeye(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n\n    # Build collective spin operators\n    Jz = 0.0\n    Jp = 0.0\n    Jm = 0.0\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Cavity operators\n    a = tensor(destroy(nmax), qeye(2**N))\n\n    # Spin operators (collective)\n    Jz_list = [tensor(qeye(nmax), *[sigmaz() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                     for i in range(N)]) / 2\n               for j in range(N)]\n    Jp_list = [tensor(qeye(nmax), *[sigmax() + 1j*sigmay() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                     for i in range(N)]) / 2\n               for j in range(N)]\n    Jm_list = [tensor(qeye(nmax), *[sigmax() - 1j*sigmay() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                     for i in range(N)]) / 2\n               for j in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * tensor(qeye(nmax),\n                                                   *[sigmaz() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                                     for i in range(N)]))\n\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * tensor(qeye(nmax),\n                                                   *[sigmaz() if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                                     for i in range(N)]))\n\n    # Build list of operators for tensor product\n    # First is cavity, rest are spins\n    cavity_ops = [destroy(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity destruction operator\n    a = tensor(cavity_ops)\n\n    # Cavity identity\n    cavity_id = tensor([qeye(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n\n    # Build collective spin operators\n    Jz = 0.0\n    Jp = 0.0\n    Jm = 0.0\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for this spin\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz = Jz + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma+\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jp = Jp + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma-\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jm = Jm + tensor(ops)\n\n    # Build list of operators for tensor product\n    # First is cavity, rest are spins\n    cavity_ops = [destroy(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity destruction operator\n    a = tensor(cavity_ops)\n\n    # Cavity identity\n    cavity_id = tensor([qeye(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n\n    # Build collective spin operators\n    Jz = 0.0\n    Jp = 0.0\n    Jm = 0.0\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for this spin\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz = Jz + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma+\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jp = Jp + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma-\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jm = Jm + tensor(ops)\n\n    # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case2(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 2: Local de-phasing & local emission (sigma-)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case3(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, gamma_up_coll, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 3: Local de-phasing & local emission & collective pumping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Local emission\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * tensor(qeye(nmax),\n                                                   *[(sigmax() - 1j*sigmay())/2 if i == j else qeye(2)\n                                                     for i in range(N)]))\n\n    # Build list of operators for tensor product\n    # First is cavity, rest are spins\n    cavity_ops = [destroy(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity destruction operator\n    a = tensor(cavity_ops)\n\n    # Cavity identity\n    cavity_id = tensor([qeye(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n\n    # Build collective spin operators\n    Jz = 0.0\n    Jp = 0.0\n    Jm = 0.0\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for this spin\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz = Jz + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma+\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jp = Jp + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case2(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 2: Local de-phasing & local emission (sigma-)\n    \"\"\"\n    # python3\n\"\"\"\nOpen Dicke Model Steady State and Wigner Function Calculation\n\nSimulates an open Dicke model with N identical two-level systems coupled to a cavity mode\nunder four different loss cases and calculates the cavity field Wigner function.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport numpy as np\nfrom qutip import *\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom itertools import product\nimport warnings\nwarnings.filterwarnings('ignore')\n\ndef setup_dicke_model(N, omega0, omegac, g, nmax):\n    \"\"\"\n    Set up the Dicke model Hamiltonian and operators.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    N : int\n        Number of two-level systems\n    omega0 : float\n        Atomic transition frequency\n    omegac : float\n        Cavity mode frequency\n    g : float\n        Coupling strength\n    nmax : int\n        Photon number cutoff\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    H : Qobj\n        Hamiltonian\n    a : Qobj\n        Cavity destruction operator\n    Jz, Jp, Jm : Qobj\n        Collective spin operators\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Build list of operators for tensor product\n    # First is cavity, rest are spins\n    cavity_ops = [destroy(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity destruction operator\n    a = tensor(cavity_ops)\n\n    # Cavity identity\n    cavity_id = tensor([qeye(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n\n    # Build collective spin operators\n    Jz = 0.0\n    Jp = 0.0\n    Jm = 0.0\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for this spin\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz = Jz + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma+\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jp = Jp + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma-\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jm = Jm + tensor(ops)\n\n    # Hamiltonian\n    H = omega0 * Jz + omegac * a.dag() * a + g * (a.dag() + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\n    # Create single spin operators for collapse operators\n    Jz_list = []\n    Jp_list = []\n    Jm_list = []\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        ops_z ops_p.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n                ops_m.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops_z.append(qeye(2))\n                ops_p.append(qeye(2))\n                ops_m.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz_list.append(tensor(ops_z))\n        Jp_list.append(tensor(ops_p))\n        Jm_list.append(tensor(ops_m))\n\n    return H, a, Jz, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case1(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_up, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 1: Local de-phasing & local pumping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local pumping (sigma+)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * Jp_list[j])\n\n    # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case2(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 2: Local de-phasing & local emission (sigma-)\n    \"\"\"\n    # for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case3(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, gamma_up_coll, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 3: Local de-phasing & local emission & collective pumping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local emission (sigma-)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Collective pumping (sigma+ for all spins)\n    Jp_coll = sum(Jp_list)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up_coll) * Jp_coll)\n\n    # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops)\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case4(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, gamma_down_coll, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 4: Local de-phasing & local emission & collective emission\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Collective emission (sigma- for all spins)\n    Jm_coll = sum(Jm_list)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down_coll) * Jm_coll)\n\n    # Solve steady state\n    rho_ss = -----------\n    rho_ss : Qobj\n        Steady state density matrix\n    nmax : int\n        Photon number cutoff\n    N : int\n        Number of spins\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Reduced cavity density matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Trace out spin degrees of freedom (keep only cavity)\n    rho_cavity = ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n    return rho_cavity\n\ndef calculate_wigner_grid(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Wigner function on a grid.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Cavity state (density matrix or -----------\n    W : 2D array\n        Wigner function values\n    xvec : array\n        x grid points\n    pvec : array\n        p grid points\n    filename : str\n        Output filename\n    \"\"\"\n    # Create DataFrame with proper indexing\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = 'p'\n    df.columns.name = 'x'\n    df.to_csv(filename)\n    print(f\"Saved {filename}\")\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Open Dicke Model - Steady State and Wigner Function Calculation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Parameters\n    N = 4\n    omega0 = Build list of operators for tensor product\n    # First is cavity, rest are spins\n    cavity_ops = [destroy(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity destruction operator\n    a = tensor(cavity_ops)\n\n    # Cavity identity\n    cavity_id = tensor([qeye(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n\n    # Build collective spin operators\n    Jz = 0.0\n    Jp = 0.0\n    Jm = 0.0\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for this spin\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz = Jz + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma+\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jp = Jp + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma-\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jm = Jm + tensor(ops)\n\n    # Hamiltonian\n    H = omega0 * Jz + omegac * a.dag() * a + g * (a.dag() + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\n    # Create single spin operators for collapse operators\n    Jz_list = []\n    Jp_list = []\n    Jm_list = []\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        ops_z = [qeye(nmax)]\n        ops_p = [qeye(nmax)]\n        ops_m = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops_z.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n                ops_p.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n                ops_m.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops_z.append(qeye(2))\n                ops_p.append(qeye(2))\n                ops_m.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz_list.append(tensor(ops_z))\n        Jp_list.append(tensor(ops_p))\n        Jm_list.append(tensor(ops_m))\n\n    return H, a, Jz, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case1(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_up, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 1: Local de-phasing & local pumping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local pumping (sigma+)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * Jp_list[j])\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case2(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 2: Local de-phasing & local emission (sigma-)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local emission (sigma-)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case3(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, gamma_up_coll, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 3: Local de-phasing & local emission & collective pumping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local emission (sigma-)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Collective pumping (sigma+ for all spins)\n    Jp_coll = sum(Jp_list)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up_coll) * Jp_coll)\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case4(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, gamma_down_coll, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 4: Local de-phasing & local emission & collective emission\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local emission (sigma-)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Collective emission (sigma- for all spins)\n    Jm_coll = sum(Jm_list)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down_coll) * Jm_coll)\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef trace_out_spins(rho_ss, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Trace out the spin degrees of freedom and extract the cavity state.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    rho_ss : Qobj\n        Steady state density matrix\n    nmax : int\n        Photon number cutoff\n    N : int\n        Number of spins\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Reduced cavity density matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Trace out spin degrees of freedom (keep only cavity)\n    rho_cavity = ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n    return rho_cavity\n\ndef calculate_wigner_grid(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Wigner function on a grid.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Cavity state (density matrix or ket)\n    xvec : array\n        x (position) grid points\n    pvec : array\n        p (momentum) grid points\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    W : 2D array\n        Wigner function values on the grid\n    \"\"\"\n    W = wigner(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec)\n    return W\n\ndef save_wigner_csv(W, xvec, pvec, filename):\n    \"\"\"\n    Save Wigner function as CSV file.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    W : 2D array\n        Wigner function values\n    xvec : array\n        x grid points\n    pvec : array\n        p grid points\n    filename : str\n        Output filename\n    \"\"\"\n    # Build list of operators for tensor product\n    # First is cavity, rest are spins\n    cavity_ops = [destroy(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)]\n\n    # Cavity destruction operator\n    a = tensor(cavity_ops)\n\n    # Cavity identity\n    cavity_id = tensor([qeye(nmax)] + [qeye(2) for _ in range(N)])\n\n    # Build collective spin operators\n    Jz = 0.0\n    Jp = 0.0\n    Jm = 0.0\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for this spin\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz = Jz + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma+\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jp = Jp + tensor(ops)\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        # Build operator list for sigma-\n        ops = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops.append(qeye(2))\n        Jm = Jm + tensor(ops)\n\n    # Hamiltonian\n    H = omega0 * Jz + omegac * a.dag() * a + g * (a.dag() + a) * (Jp + Jm)\n\n    # Create single spin operators for collapse operators\n    Jz_list = []\n    Jp_list = []\n    Jm_list = []\n\n    for j in range(N):\n        ops_z = [qeye(nmax)]\n        ops_p = [qeye(nmax)]\n        ops_m = [qeye(nmax)]\n        for i in range(N):\n            if i == j:\n                ops_z.append(sigmaz() / 2)\n                ops_p.append((sigmax() + 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n                ops_m.append((sigmax() - 1j*sigmay()) / 2)\n            else:\n                ops_z.append(qeye(2))\n                ops_p.append(qeye(2))\n                ops_m.append(qeye(2))\n        Jz_list.append(tensor(ops_z))\n        Jp_list.append(tensor(ops_p))\n        Jm_list.append(tensor(ops_m))\n\n    return H, a, Jz, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case1(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_up, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 1: Local de-phasing & local pumping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local pumping (sigma+)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up) * Jp_list[j])\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case2(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 2: Local de-phasing & local emission (sigma-)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local emission (sigma-)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case3(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, gamma_up_coll, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 3: Local de-phasing & local emission & collective pumping\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local emission (sigma-)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Collective pumping (sigma+ for all spins)\n    Jp_coll = sum(Jp_list)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_up_coll) * Jp_coll)\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef compute_steady_state_case4(H, a, Jp, Jm, Jz_list, Jp_list, Jm_list, kappa, gamma_phi, gamma_down, gamma_down_coll, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Case 4: Local de-phasing & local emission & collective emission\n    \"\"\"\n    # Cavity loss\n    c_ops = [np.sqrt(kappa) * a]\n\n    # Local dephasing (sigmaz)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_phi) * Jz_list[j] * 2)\n\n    # Local emission (sigma-)\n    for j in range(N):\n        c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down) * Jm_list[j])\n\n    # Collective emission (sigma- for all spins)\n    Jm_coll = sum(Jm_list)\n    c_ops.append(np.sqrt(gamma_down_coll) * Jm_coll)\n\n    # Solve steady state with improved method\n    try:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='direct')\n    except:\n        rho_ss = steadystate(H, c_ops, method='eigen')\n    return rho_ss\n\ndef trace_out_spins(rho_ss, nmax, N):\n    \"\"\"\n    Trace out the spin degrees of freedom and extract the cavity state.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    rho_ss : Qobj\n        Steady state density matrix\n    nmax : int\n        Photon number cutoff\n    N : int\n        Number of spins\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Reduced cavity density matrix\n    \"\"\"\n    # Trace out spin degrees of freedom (keep only cavity)\n    rho_cavity = ptrace(rho_ss, 0)\n    return rho_cavity\n\ndef calculate_wigner_grid(rho_cavity, xvec, pvec):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Wigner function on a grid using optimized method.\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    rho_cavity : Qobj\n        Cavity state (density matrix or ket)\n    xvec : array\n        x (position) grid points\n    pvec : array\n        p (momentum) grid points\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    W : 2D array\n        Wigner function values on the grid\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"  Calculating Wigner function... Create DataFrame with proper indexing\n    df = pd.DataFrame(W, index=pvec, columns=xvec)\n    df.index.name = 'p'\n    df.columns.name = 'x'\n    df.to_csv(filename)\n    print(f\"Saved {filename}\")\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"Open Dicke Model - Steady State and Wigner Function Calculation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    #
